Test Implementation:
func TestNodeMessageLoopClient_Recv_Success_005(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockStream := new(grpc.ClientStream)
    serverMsg := &ServerMsg{}
    mockStream.On("RecvMsg", mock.Anything).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
        *args.Get(0).(**ServerMsg) = serverMsg
    }).Return(nil)
    client := &nodeMessageLoopClient{ClientStream: mockStream}

    // Act
    msg, err := client.Recv()

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    require.NotNil(t, msg)
    assert.Equal(t, serverMsg, msg)
    mockStream.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestInit_ValidConfigDisabled_321(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    handler.initialized = false
    jsonConfig := json.RawMessage(`{"enabled": false, "buffer": 10}`)

    // Act
    success, err := handler.Init(jsonConfig)

    // Assert
    require.False(t, success)
    require.NoError(t, err)
    assert.Nil(t, handler.input)
    assert.Nil(t, handler.channel)
    assert.Nil(t, handler.stop)
}

import (

	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
)

Test Implementation:


func TestNodeClient_MessageLoop_Success_456(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockConn := new(MockClientConn)
	mockStream := new(MockClientStream)
	ctx := context.Background()
	client := NewNodeClient(mockConn)

	mockConn.On("NewStream", ctx, &Node_ServiceDesc.Streams[0], "/pbx.Node/MessageLoop", mock.AnythingOfType("[]grpc.CallOption")).Return(mockStream, nil)

	// Act
	streamClient, err := client.MessageLoop(ctx)

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, streamClient)
	assert.IsType(t, &nodeMessageLoopClient{}, streamClient)
	concreteStreamClient, ok := streamClient.(*nodeMessageLoopClient)
	require.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, mockStream, concreteStreamClient.ClientStream)
	mockConn.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Import Statements:
import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:


func TestNodeMessageLoopClient_Recv_Success_131(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockStream := new(MockClientStream)
	client := &nodeMessageLoopClient{mockStream}
	expectedMsg := &ServerMsg{Message: &ServerMsg_Ctrl{Ctrl: &ServerCtrl{Id: "tst"}}} // Example message

	// Use Run to populate the message passed to RecvMsg
	mockStream.On("RecvMsg", mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ServerMsg")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		msg := args.Get(0).(*ServerMsg)
		*msg = *expectedMsg // Copy the expected message content
	}).Return(nil)

	// Act
	msg, err := client.Recv()

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg)
	// Use assert.Equal to compare the received message with the expected one.
	// Proto messages might require specific comparison methods if assert.Equal doesn't work directly.
	// For basic checks:
	assert.Equal(t, expectedMsg.GetCtrl().GetId(), msg.GetCtrl().GetId())
	mockStream.AssertExpectations(t)
}

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"
)

Test Implementation:


func TestUnimplementedNodeServer_MessageLoop_Unimplemented_151(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	server := UnimplementedNodeServer{}
	mockStream := new(MockNodeMessageLoopServer) // Use a concrete mock if needed, or nil if interface allows

	// Act
	err := server.MessageLoop(mockStream) // Pass the mock stream

	// Assert
	require.Error(t, err)
	st, ok := status.FromError(err)
	require.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, codes.Unimplemented, st.Code())
	assert.Equal(t, "method MessageLoop not implemented", st.Message())
}

func TestNodeMessageLoopServer_Recv_Success_221(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockStream := new(MockServerStream)
	server := &nodeMessageLoopServer{mockStream}
	expectedMsg := &ClientMsg{Message: &ClientMsg_Hi{Hi: &ClientHi{Id: "tstClient"}}} // Example client message

	// Use Run to populate the message passed to RecvMsg
	mockStream.On("RecvMsg", mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ClientMsg")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		msg := args.Get(0).(*ClientMsg)
		*msg = *expectedMsg // Copy the expected message content
	}).Return(nil)

	// Act
	msg, err := server.Recv()

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg)
	assert.Equal(t, expectedMsg.GetHi().GetId(), msg.GetHi().GetId())
	mockStream.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Test Implementation:


func TestPluginClient_FireHose_Success_251(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockConn := new(MockClientConn)
	client := NewPluginClient(mockConn)
	ctx := context.Background()
	in := &ClientReq{Msg: &ClientMsg{Message: &ClientMsg_Hi{Hi: &ClientHi{Id: "req"}}}}
	expectedOut := &ServerResp{Status: RespCode_OK}

	// Expect Invoke to be called and return success
	// Note: We pass mock.Anything for the 'reply' argument because Invoke modifies it in place.
	// The mock return value (expectedOut) isn't directly used to populate reply here.
	// Instead, we assert the returned value from the FireHose call matches expectedOut.
	mockConn.On("Invoke", ctx, "/pbx.Plugin/FireHose", in, mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ServerResp"), mock.AnythingOfType("[]grpc.CallOption")).
		Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
			// Simulate populating the reply object
			reply := args.Get(3).(*ServerResp)
			*reply = *expectedOut
		}).
		Return(nil) // Return nil error for success

	// Act
	out, err := client.FireHose(ctx, in)

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, out)
	assert.Equal(t, expectedOut.Status, out.Status) // Compare relevant fields
	mockConn.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestNodeClient_MessageLoop_Success_002(t *testing.T) {

	mockStream := new(MockClientStream)

	mockConn.On("NewStream", mock.Anything, &Node_ServiceDesc.Streams[0], "/pbx.Node/MessageLoop", opts).Return(mockStream, nil)

	server := UnimplementedNodeServer{}
	mockStream := new(MockNodeMessageLoopServer) // Use the defined mock

	// Act
	err := server.MessageLoop(mockStream) // Pass the mock stream

	// Assert
	require.Error(t, err)
	st, ok := status.FromError(err)
	require.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, codes.Unimplemented, st.Code())
	assert.Equal(t, "method MessageLoop not implemented", st.Message())
	// No expectations to assert on the mock stream as it shouldn't be used
}

Test Implementation:
func TestNodeMessageLoopServer_Recv_Success_014(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockStream := new(MockServerStream)
	server := &nodeMessageLoopServer{mockStream}
	expectedMsg := &ClientMsg{Message: &ClientMsg_Hi{Hi: &ClientHi{Id: "tstClient"}}} // Example client message

	// Use Run to populate the message passed to RecvMsg
	mockStream.On("RecvMsg", mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ClientMsg")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		msg := args.Get(0).(*ClientMsg)
		*msg = *expectedMsg // Copy the expected message content
	}).Return(nil)

	// Act
	msg, err := server.Recv()

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, msg)
	assert.Equal(t, expectedMsg.GetHi().GetId(), msg.GetHi().GetId())
	mockStream.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestPluginClient_FireHose_Success_017(t *testing.T) {

	mockConn := new(MockClientConnInterface)
	client := NewPluginClient(mockConn)
	ctx := context.Background()
	in := &ClientReq{Msg: &ClientMsg{Message: &ClientMsg_Hi{Hi: &ClientHi{Id: "req"}}}}
	expectedOut := &ServerResp{Status: RespCode_OK} // Use pbx.RespCode_OK
	var opts []grpc.CallOption

	mockConn.On("Invoke", mock.Anything, "/pbx.Plugin/FireHose", in, mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ServerResp"), opts).
		Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
			reply := args.Get(3).(*ServerResp)
			*reply = *expectedOut
		}).
		Return(nil) // Return nil error for success

	// Act
	out, err := client.FireHose(ctx, in, opts...)

	// Assert
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotNil(t, out)
	assert.Equal(t, expectedOut.Status, out.Status) // Compare relevant fields
	mockConn.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestChannelSubUnsub_Subscription_123(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockPush := new(pushMocks.Push)
    topic := &Topic{name: "test-topic"}
    uid := types.ParseUid("usr123")
    sub := true

    mockPush.On("ChannelSub", &push.ChannelReq{
        Uid:     uid,
        Channel: types.GrpToChn(topic.name),
        Unsub:   !sub,
    }).Return(nil)

    // Act
    topic.channelSubUnsub(uid, sub)

    // Assert
    mockPush.AssertCalled(t, "ChannelSub", &push.ChannelReq{
        Uid:     uid,
        Channel: types.GrpToChn(topic.name),
        Unsub:   !sub,
    })
    mockPush.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestPushForData_ValidData_456(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    topic := &Topic{name: "test-topic", isChan: false, perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
        types.ParseUid("usr123"): {online: 1, modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
    }}
    data := &MsgServerData{
        From:      "usr456",
        Timestamp: time.Now(),
        SeqId:     10,
        Head: map[string]interface{}{
            "mime":    "text/plain",
            "webrtc":  "active",
            "aonly":   true,
            "replace": "seq123",
        },
        Content: "Hello, World!",
    }
    msgMarkedAsReadBySender := false

    // Act
    receipt := topic.pushForData(types.ParseUid("usr123"), data, msgMarkedAsReadBySender)

    // Assert
    require.NotNil(t, receipt)
    assert.Equal(t, push.ActMsg, receipt.Payload.What)
    assert.Equal(t, "test-topic", receipt.Payload.Topic)
    assert.Equal(t, "usr456", receipt.Payload.From)
    assert.Equal(t, "text/plain", receipt.Payload.ContentType)
    assert.Equal(t, "Hello, World!", receipt.Payload.Content)
    assert.Equal(t, "active", receipt.Payload.Webrtc)
    assert.True(t, receipt.Payload.AudioOnly)
    assert.Equal(t, "seq123", receipt.Payload.Replace)
    assert.Equal(t, 1, receipt.To[types.ParseUid("usr123")].Delivered)
    assert.True(t, receipt.To[types.ParseUid("usr123")].ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache)
}

func TestPushForGroupSub_GroupSubscription_789(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    topic := &Topic{
        name:      "test-group",
        xoriginal: "grp123",
        cat:       types.TopicCatGroup,
        perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
            types.ParseUid("usr123"): {modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
            types.ParseUid("usr456"): {modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
        },
    }
    now := time.Now()

    // Act
    receipt := topic.pushForGroupSub(types.ParseUid("usr123"), now)

    // Assert
    require.NotNil(t, receipt)
    assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receipt.Payload.What)
    assert.Equal(t, "grp123", receipt.Payload.Topic)
    assert.Equal(t, "usr123", receipt.Payload.From)
    assert.Equal(t, now, receipt.Payload.Timestamp)
    assert.Equal(t, types.ModeJoin, receipt.Payload.ModeWant)
    assert.Equal(t, types.ModeJoin, receipt.Payload.ModeGiven)
    assert.Contains(t, receipt.To, types.ParseUid("usr456"))
}

)

Test Implementation:
func TestNodeMessageLoopClient_Recv_Success_106(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    mockStream := new(MockClientStream)
    client := &nodeMessageLoopClient{mockStream}
    // Use an actual ServerMsg instance
    expectedMsg := &ServerMsg{Message: &ServerMsg_Ctrl{Ctrl: &ServerCtrl{Id: "tst"}}}

    // Use Run to populate the message passed to RecvMsg
    mockStream.On("RecvMsg", mock.AnythingOfType("*pbx.ServerMsg")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
        msg := args.Get(0).(*ServerMsg)
        // Copy the expected message content
        // Make sure to handle potential nil pointers if copying nested structs
        if expectedMsg.GetCtrl() != nil {
            msg.Message = &ServerMsg_Ctrl{Ctrl: &ServerCtrl{Id: expectedMsg.GetCtrl().GetId()}}
        }
    }).Return(nil)

    // Act
    msg, err := client.Recv()

    // Assert
    require.NoError(t, err)
    require.NotNil(t, msg)
    require.NotNil(t, msg.GetCtrl(), "Received message should have Ctrl field")
    assert.Equal(t, expectedMsg.GetCtrl().GetId(), msg.GetCtrl().GetId())
    mockStream.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestChannelSubUnsub_Subscription_001(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    uid := types.ParseUid("usr123")
    sub := true
    topic := &Topic{name: "test-topic"}

    mockPush := new(pushMocks.Push)
    mockPush.On("ChannelSub", &push.ChannelReq{
        Uid:     uid,
        Channel: types.GrpToChn(topic.name),
        Unsub:   !sub,
    }).Return(nil)

    // Act
    push.ChannelSub = mockPush.ChannelSub
    topic.channelSubUnsub(uid, sub)

    // Assert
    mockPush.AssertCalled(t, "ChannelSub", &push.ChannelReq{
        Uid:     uid,
        Channel: types.GrpToChn(topic.name),
        Unsub:   !sub,
    })
    mockPush.AssertExpectations(t)
}

import (
    "testing"
    "time"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
    "github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"
)

Required Library Installation
go get github.com/stretchr/testify

Test Implementation:
func TestPushForData_ValidData_002(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    topic := &Topic{
        name: "test-topic",
        isChan: false,
        perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
            types.ParseUid("usr123"): {online: 1, modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
        },
    }
    data := &MsgServerData{
        From:      "usr456",
        Timestamp: time.Now(),
        SeqId:     10,
        Head: map[string]interface{}{
            "mime":    "text/plain",
            "webrtc":  "active",
            "aonly":   true,
            "replace": "seq123",
        },
        Content: "Hello, World!",
    }
    msgMarkedAsReadBySender := false

    // Act
    receipt := topic.pushForData(types.ParseUid("usr123"), data, msgMarkedAsReadBySender)

    // Assert
    require.NotNil(t, receipt)
    assert.Equal(t, push.ActMsg, receipt.Payload.What)
    assert.Equal(t, "test-topic", receipt.Payload.Topic)
    assert.Equal(t, "usr456", receipt.Payload.From)
    assert.Equal(t, "text/plain", receipt.Payload.ContentType)
    assert.Equal(t, "Hello, World!", receipt.Payload.Content)
    assert.Equal(t, "active", receipt.Payload.Webrtc)
    assert.True(t, receipt.Payload.AudioOnly)
    assert.Equal(t, "seq123", receipt.Payload.Replace)
    assert.Equal(t, 1, receipt.To[types.ParseUid("usr123")].Delivered)
    assert.True(t, receipt.To[types.ParseUid("usr123")].ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache)
}

func TestPushForGroupSub_GroupSubscription_003(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    topic := &Topic{
        name:      "test-group",
        xoriginal: "grp123",
        cat:       types.TopicCatGroup,
        perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
            types.ParseUid("usr123"): {modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
            types.ParseUid("usr456"): {modeWant: types.ModeJoin, modeGiven: types.ModeJoin, deleted: false, isChan: false},
        },
    }
    now := time.Now()

    // Act
    receipt := topic.pushForGroupSub(types.ParseUid("usr123"), now)

    // Assert
    require.NotNil(t, receipt)
    assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receipt.Payload.What)
    assert.Equal(t, "grp123", receipt.Payload.Topic)
    assert.Equal(t, "usr123", receipt.Payload.From)
    assert.Equal(t, now, receipt.Payload.Timestamp)
    assert.Equal(t, types.ModeJoin, receipt.Payload.ModeWant)
    assert.Equal(t, types.ModeJoin, receipt.Payload.ModeGiven)
    assert.Contains(t, receipt.To, types.ParseUid("usr456"))
}

func TestServeWebSocket_ValidAPIKey_123(t *testing.T) {
    // Arrange
    req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
    req.Header.Set("X-API-Key", "valid-api-key")
    wrt := httptest.NewRecorder()

    mockGlobals := &Globals{
        sessionStore: NewMockSessionStore(),
        useXForwardedFor: true,
    }

    globals = mockGlobals

    mockGlobals.sessionStore.On("NewSession", mock.Anything, "").Return(&Session{sid: "test-session-id"}, 1)

    // Act
    serveWebSocket(wrt, req)

    // Assert

    mockGlobals.sessionStore.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestSession_SendMessage_Success_334(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  resetStats()
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
    sid:  "testSid",
    ws:   mockWs,
    send: make(chan interface{}, 1), // Ensure channel has buffer
  }
  msgBytes := []byte("test message")

  // Mock wsWrite directly as it's a standalone function
  originalWsWrite := wsWrite
  wsWrite = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
      assert.Equal(t, mockWs, ws)
      assert.Equal(t, websocket.TextMessage, mt)
      assert.Equal(t, msgBytes, msg.([]byte))
      return nil
  }
  defer func() { wsWrite = originalWsWrite }() // Restore original

  // Act
  result := sess.sendMessage(msgBytes)

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, result)
  assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
}

func TestSession_SendMessage_QueueLimitExceeded_742(t *testing.T) {

    sid:  "testSid",

  }
  // Fill the send channel to exceed the limit
  for i := 0; i < sendQueueLimit+1; i++ {
    sess.send <- "dummy message"
  }

  // Act
  result := sess.sendMessage([]byte("test message"))

  // Assert
  assert.False(t, result)

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
	"testing"
	"time"
    "errors"

	"github.com/gorilla/websocket"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_ReadLoop_Success_909(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  resetStats()
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
    sid: "testSid",
    ws:  mockWs,
    // Mock dependencies for cleanUp and dispatchRaw if necessary
    // For simplicity, assume they don't cause issues here
  }

  // Mock cleanUp to prevent actual cleanup during test
  originalCleanUp := sess.cleanUp
  sess.cleanUp = func(evicted bool) {}
  defer func() { sess.cleanUp = originalCleanUp }()

  // Mock dispatchRaw
  var dispatchedData []byte
  originalDispatchRaw := sess.dispatchRaw
  sess.dispatchRaw = func(rawData []byte) {
       dispatchedData = rawData
       // Simulate error to break loop after one iteration
       panic(assert.AnError)
  }
  defer func() { sess.dispatchRaw = originalDispatchRaw }()


  msgBytes := []byte(`{"id":"123","hi":{"id":"hi123"}}`)
  // First call: return message
  mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(websocket.TextMessage, msgBytes, nil).Once()
  // Second call: return error to stop the loop
  mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, assert.AnError).Once()

  mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return()
  mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Twice() // Initial + after pong
  mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.AnythingOfType("func(string) error")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
          // Simulate receiving a pong
          handler := args.Get(0).(func(string) error)
          err := handler("pong data")
          assert.NoError(t, err)
  }).Return()
  mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil) // Expected during defer

  // Act & Assert
  // Use assert.Panics because we force an error in dispatchRaw to exit loop
   assert.PanicsWithError(t, assert.AnError.Error(), func() {
         sess.readLoop()
   })


  // Assert further conditions after the loop finishes (or panics)
  mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
  assert.Equal(t, msgBytes, dispatchedData)
  assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
}

func TestSession_ReadLoop_NormalClose_378(t *testing.T) {

  resetStats()

  sess := &Session{
    sid: "testSid",

  }

    cleanedUp = true
    assert.False(t, evicted)
  }

  // Normal close is NOT an "unexpected" close error
  closeErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseNormalClosure, Text: "client closed"}

  mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return()
  mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil)
  mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return()
  mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, closeErr) // Return normal close error
  mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil)                    // Expected from defer

  // Act
  sess.readLoop()

  // Assert
  mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
  assert.True(t, cleanedUp)
  assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
   // No error should be logged for normal closure
}

import (

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendBatch_012(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour), // Non-firing timer
  }
  // Mock serializeAndUpdateStats and sendMessage
  serializedMsg1 := []byte("msg1")
  serializedMsg2 := []byte("msg2")

  sess.serializeAndUpdateStats = func(msg *ServerComMessage) []byte {
      if msg.Ctrl != nil && msg.Ctrl.Id == "msg1" { return serializedMsg1}
      if msg.Ctrl != nil && msg.Ctrl.Id == "msg2" { return serializedMsg2}
      return nil
  }

  sendCount := 0
  sess.sendMessage = func(msg interface{}) bool {
      sendCount++
      if sendCount == 1 {
          assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg1, msg.([]byte))
      } else {
          assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg2, msg.([]byte))
      }
      return true // Simulate successful send
  }

  // Use a timer that won't fire automatically
  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      // Send batch message
       msg1 := &ServerComMessage{Ctrl: &MsgServerCtrl{Id: "msg1"}}
       msg2 := &ServerComMessage{Ctrl: &MsgServerCtrl{Id: "msg2"}}
      sess.send <- []*ServerComMessage{msg1, msg2}
       // Stop the loop
       time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond) // Give time for send to be processed
       sess.stop <- nil
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.Equal(t, 2, sendCount) // Both messages should be sent
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close") // Ensure connection closed on exit
  // Ticker stop is handled internally
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendSerialized_119(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }
  serializedMsg := []byte("serialized data")

  // No need to mock serializeAndUpdateStats for this case

  sent := false
  sess.sendMessage = func(msg interface{}) bool {
      assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg, msg.([]byte))
      sent = true
      return true
  }

  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

  go func() {
      sess.send <- serializedMsg
      time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
      sess.stop <- nil
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, sent)
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendMessageFail_011(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Need stop channel to avoid deadlock if send fails
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }
  serializedMsg := []byte("serialized data")

  sent := false
  sess.sendMessage = func(msg interface{}) bool {
      assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg, msg.([]byte))
      sent = true
      return false // Simulate send failure
  }

   originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
   mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
   time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
       return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
   }
   defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

  go func() {
      sess.send <- serializedMsg
       // No need to send stop, loop should exit on send failure
       time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond) // Wait a bit longer
       // Safety stop if the loop doesn't exit as expected
       select {
        case sess.stop <- nil:
        default:
       }
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, sent) // Should attempt to send
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close") // Should close on exit
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_BackgroundTimer_372(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  mockBkgTimerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  &time.Timer{C: mockBkgTimerChan},
      background: true, // Start in background
  }

  onBackgroundTimerCalled := false
  sess.onBackgroundTimer = func() {
      onBackgroundTimerCalled = true
  }

  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      mockBkgTimerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger background timer
      time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
      sess.stop <- nil // Stop the loop
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, onBackgroundTimerCalled)
  assert.False(t, sess.background) // Should be set to false
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_StopWithMessage_991(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }
  stopMsg := []byte("stopping")

  // Mock wsWrite for the stop message
  wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg := false
  originalWsWrite := wsWrite
  wsWrite = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
       if bytes, ok := msg.([]byte); ok && string(bytes) == string(stopMsg) {
           assert.Equal(t, websocket.TextMessage, mt)
           wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg = true
       }
       // Ignore ping writes for simplicity in this test
       if mt == websocket.PingMessage { return nil}

       return nil // Assume other writes succeed or don't happen
  }
  defer func() { wsWrite = originalWsWrite }()


  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      sess.stop <- stopMsg // Send stop signal with message
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg, "wsWrite should be called with the stop message")
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_StopWithNil_738(t *testing.T) {

  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }

  // Mock wsWrite to ensure it's NOT called with TextMessage
  wsWriteCalledWithText := false
  originalWsWrite := wsWrite
  wsWrite = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
       if mt == websocket.TextMessage {
           wsWriteCalledWithText = true
       }
        // Ignore ping writes for simplicity in this test
       if mt == websocket.PingMessage { return nil}
       return nil
  }
  defer func() { wsWrite = originalWsWrite }()


  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      sess.stop <- nil // Send stop signal with nil
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.False(t, wsWriteCalledWithText, "wsWrite should not be called with TextMessage for nil stop")
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_Detach_512(t *testing.T) {

      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
      subs:      make(map[string]*Subscription), // Ensure subs map exists
  }
  topicToDetach := "grpTopic123"

  delSubCalledWith := ""
  sess.delSub = func(topic string) {
      delSubCalledWith = topic
  }

  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      sess.detach <- topicToDetach // Send detach signal
      time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
      sess.stop <- nil // Stop the loop
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.Equal(t, topicToDetach, delSubCalledWith)
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingSuccess_681(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }

  pingWriteCalled := false
  originalWsWrite := wsWrite
  wsWrite = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
      if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
          pingWriteCalled = true
          assert.Nil(t, msg)
          return nil // Simulate successful ping write
      }
       // Ignore TextMessage writes for simplicity
      if mt == websocket.TextMessage { return nil}
      return nil
  }
  defer func() { wsWrite = originalWsWrite }()


  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      // Use the actual pingPeriod for realism, though it doesn't affect mock logic
      assert.Equal(t, pingPeriod, d)
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      mockTickerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger ticker
      time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
      sess.stop <- nil // Stop the loop
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, pingWriteCalled)
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingFailNormal_883(t *testing.T) {

  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  sess := &Session{
      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Need stop to prevent deadlock if ping fails
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }
  expectedErr := errors.New("ping write failed")

  pingWriteAttempted := false
  originalWsWrite := wsWrite
  wsWrite = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
      if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
          pingWriteAttempted = true
          return expectedErr // Simulate ping write failure
      }
       // Ignore TextMessage writes for simplicity
       if mt == websocket.TextMessage { return nil}
      return nil
  }
  defer func() { wsWrite = originalWsWrite }()


  originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
  mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
  time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
      return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
  }
  defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


  go func() {
      mockTickerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger ticker
       // Loop should exit on ping error, no explicit stop needed
        time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond) // Wait a bit longer
       // Safety stop if the loop doesn't exit as expected
       select {
        case sess.stop <- nil:
        default:
       }
  }()

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  assert.True(t, pingWriteAttempted)
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close") // Should close on exit after error
  // Log check for non-unexpected error omitted
}

      sid:       "testSid",
      ws:        mockWs,
      send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
      stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
      detach:    make(chan string, 1),
      bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
  }

   originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
   mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time) // Block ticker
   time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
       return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
   }
   defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

  // Close the send channel before starting the loop
  close(sess.send)

  // Act
  sess.writeLoop()

  // Assert
  // Loop should exit immediately upon detecting closed channel
  mockWs.AssertCalled(t, "Close")
}

  mockCheckAPIKeyErr = nil

  mockUpgrader := new(mockUpgrader)
  upgrader = *mockUpgrader // Assign the mock instance
  defer func() { upgrader = originalUpgrader }() // Restore original

  handshakeErr := websocket.HandshakeError{Description: "bad handshake"}
  mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(nil, handshakeErr)

  // Act
  serveWebSocket(rr, req)

  // Assert
  mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
  // ResponseWriter status is often set by the upgrader itself in handshake errors
   // We mainly care that our function exited correctly and logged.
   // Log check omitted.
}

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_NoXFF_451(t *testing.T) {
  // Arrange
  req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
  req.RemoteAddr = "192.0.2.1:12345" // Set remote addr
  rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

  mockGetAPIKeyResult = "validKey"
  mockCheckAPIKeyResult = true
  mockCheckAPIKeyErr = nil
  globals.useXForwardedFor = false // Ensure XFF is disabled

  originalUpgrader := upgrader
  mockUpgrader := new(mockUpgrader)
  upgrader = *mockUpgrader
  defer func() { upgrader = originalUpgrader }()

  mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
  // Cast mockWs to satisfy websocket.Conn interface expected by Upgrade
  var connPtr *websocket.Conn = (*websocket.Conn)(mockWs)
  mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(connPtr, nil)


  // Mock session store
  mockStore := new(mockSessionStore)
  globals.sessionStore = mockStore // Replace global store

  sess := &Session{sid: "newSessionID"} // Session to be returned by store
  expectedCount := 5

  mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.AnythingOfType("*websocket.Conn"), "").Return(sess, expectedCount)

  // Mock loops to prevent them from actually running and blocking
  originalWriteLoop := sess.writeLoop
  originalReadLoop := sess.readLoop
  writeLoopCalled := false
  readLoopCalled := false
  sess.writeLoop = func() { writeLoopCalled = true }
  sess.readLoop = func() { readLoopCalled = true }
  defer func() { sess.writeLoop = originalWriteLoop; sess.readLoop = originalReadLoop }()


  // Act
  serveWebSocket(rr, req)

  // Assert
  mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
  mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)

  // Verify session properties
  assert.Equal(t, req.RemoteAddr, sess.remoteAddr)

   // Give goroutines a chance to start (though they are mocked)
   time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
   assert.True(t, writeLoopCalled, "writeLoop should have been called")
   assert.True(t, readLoopCalled, "readLoop should have been called")

}

// Mocking push.ChannelSub is complex without refactoring.
// This test primarily ensures the function executes without panic and covers the lines.
// It relies on the actual push.ChannelSub potentially making external calls or logging.
func TestChannelSubUnsub_Subscribe_123(t *testing.T) {
  topicName := "grpTestTopic123"
  uid := types.ParseUserId("usrTestUser123")
  tp := &Topic{
    name: topicName,
  }

  // We expect push.ChannelSub to be called, but cannot easily assert without mocks/refactor.
  // If push.ChannelSub logs, check logs. If it panics, the test fails.
  assert.NotPanics(t, func() {
    tp.channelSubUnsub(uid, true) // Subscribe
  })

  // Minimal assertion: check if GrpToChn conversion is correct indirectly
  // (though the real check happens inside push.ChannelSub)
  expectedChannel := types.GrpToChn(topicName)
  assert.NotEmpty(t, expectedChannel) // Basic sanity check
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
  "testing"
  "time"

  "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
  "github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
  "github.com/tinode/chat/server/push"
  "github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"
)

Test Implementation:


func TestPushForData_GroupTopic_789(t *testing.T) {
  topicName := "grpDataTopic789"
  fromUid := types.ParseUserId("usrSender789")
  toUid1 := types.ParseUserId("usrRcpt1") // Valid recipient
  toUid2 := types.ParseUserId("usrRcpt2") // Deleted recipient
  toUid3 := types.ParseUserId("usrRcpt3") // Invalid mode recipient
  toUid4 := types.ParseUserId("usrRcpt4") // Valid recipient, offline sender
  senderUid := fromUid                     // Sender

  now := types.TimeNow()
  msgData := &MsgServerData{
    Head: map[string]any{
      "mime":   "text/plain",
      "webrtc": "accepted", // Test webrtc header
      "aonly":  true,       // Test aonly header
      "replace": "some-seq",  // Test replace header
    },
    From:      fromUid.UserId(),
    Timestamp: now,
    SeqId:     123,
    Content:   "hello world",
  }

  tp := &Topic{
    name: topicName,
    cat:  types.TopicCatGrp,
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
      toUid1: { // Valid: Presencer + Reader, not deleted, not channel sub
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid2: { // Invalid: Deleted
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   true,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid3: { // Invalid: Not Presencer
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      senderUid: { // Sender - offline initially
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres | types.ModeWrite,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres | types.ModeWrite,
        online:    0, // Should be treated as online=1 for silent push
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
       toUid4: { // Valid: Presencer + Reader, not deleted, not channel sub
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    0, // Delivery count 0
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
    },
  }

  // Scenario 1: Sender read the message
  receipt := tp.pushForData(fromUid, msgData, true)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt)
  assert.Empty(t, receipt.Channel) // Not a channel topic
  assert.Len(t, receipt.To, 3)      // Rcpt1, Rcpt4, Sender

  // Check Rcpt1
  rcpt1, ok := receipt.To[toUid1]
  assert.True(t, ok)
  assert.Equal(t, 1, rcpt1.Delivered)
  assert.True(t, rcpt1.ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache) // Not sender

  // Check Rcpt4
  rcpt4, ok := receipt.To[toUid4]
  assert.True(t, ok)
  assert.Equal(t, 0, rcpt4.Delivered) // Was offline
  assert.True(t, rcpt4.ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache) // Not sender

  // Check Sender
  senderRcpt, ok := receipt.To[senderUid]
  assert.True(t, ok)
  assert.Equal(t, 1, senderRcpt.Delivered) // Treated as online=1
  assert.False(t, senderRcpt.ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache) // Sender read it

  // Check Payload
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActMsg, receipt.Payload.What)
  assert.False(t, receipt.Payload.Silent)
  assert.Equal(t, topicName, receipt.Payload.Topic)
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receipt.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receipt.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, 123, receipt.Payload.SeqId)
  assert.Equal(t, "text/plain", receipt.Payload.ContentType)
  assert.Equal(t, "hello world", receipt.Payload.Content)
  assert.Equal(t, "accepted", receipt.Payload.Webrtc)
  assert.True(t, receipt.Payload.AudioOnly)
  assert.Equal(t, "some-seq", receipt.Payload.Replace)


  // Scenario 2: Sender did NOT read the message
  receipt2 := tp.pushForData(fromUid, msgData, false)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt2)
  assert.Len(t, receipt2.To, 3) // Rcpt1, Rcpt4, Sender
  senderRcpt2, ok := receipt2.To[senderUid]
  assert.True(t, ok)
  assert.Equal(t, 1, senderRcpt2.Delivered)
  assert.True(t, senderRcpt2.ShouldIncrementUnreadCountInCache) // Sender did NOT read it

  // Scenario 3: No valid recipients
  tp.perUser = map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
     toUid2: { // Invalid: Deleted
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   true,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid3: { // Invalid: Not Presencer
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
  }
  receipt3 := tp.pushForData(fromUid, msgData, false)
  assert.Nil(t, receipt3)

}

import (
  "testing"
  "time"

  "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"

Test Implementation:


func TestPushForData_ChannelTopic_012(t *testing.T) {
  topicName := "grpChanTopic012"
  fromUid := types.ParseUserId("usrChanSender012")
  toUid1 := types.ParseUserId("usrChanRcpt1") // Valid non-channel sub
  toUid2 := types.ParseUserId("usrChanRcpt2") // Invalid channel sub

  now := types.TimeNow()
  msgData := &MsgServerData{
    Head:      map[string]any{"mime": "text/plain"},
    From:      fromUid.UserId(),
    Timestamp: now,
    SeqId:     456,
    Content:   "channel message",
  }

  tp := &Topic{
    name:   topicName,
    cat:    types.TopicCatGrp,
    isChan: true, // This makes it a channel topic
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
      toUid1: { // Valid: Presencer + Reader, not deleted, not channel sub
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false, // Non-channel subscription means send individually
      },
      toUid2: { // Invalid: Is a channel subscription (should receive via FCM topic)
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    true, // Is a channel subscription
      },
       fromUid: { // Sender
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres | types.ModeWrite,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres | types.ModeWrite,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
    },
  }

  receipt := tp.pushForData(fromUid, msgData, false)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt)
  assert.Equal(t, types.GrpToChn(topicName), receipt.Channel) // Channel name must be set
  assert.Len(t, receipt.To, 2)                       // Only Rcpt1 and Sender (individual subs)

  _, ok1 := receipt.To[toUid1]
  assert.True(t, ok1)
  _, okSender := receipt.To[fromUid]
  assert.True(t, okSender)
  _, ok2 := receipt.To[toUid2]
  assert.False(t, ok2) // Rcpt2 should not be in To map

  // Payload checks
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActMsg, receipt.Payload.What)
  assert.Equal(t, topicName, receipt.Payload.Topic) // Topic name remains original grp name
}

func TestPreparePushForSubReceipt_Scenarios_345(t *testing.T) {
  fromUid := types.ParseUserId("usrSubSender345")
  now := types.TimeNow()
  lastID := 5

  // Scenario 1: P2P Topic
  p2pTopic := &Topic{
    cat:       types.TopicCatP2P,
    xoriginal: "usrOtherUser", // Should be ignored for P2P
    lastID:    lastID,
     perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{}, // Needed for subsCount
  }
  receiptP2P := p2pTopic.preparePushForSubReceipt(fromUid, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receiptP2P)
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receiptP2P.Payload.What)
  assert.False(t, receiptP2P.Payload.Silent)
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receiptP2P.Payload.Topic) // Topic should be sender's ID for P2P
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receiptP2P.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receiptP2P.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, lastID, receiptP2P.Payload.SeqId)
  assert.NotNil(t, receiptP2P.To)
  assert.Empty(t, receiptP2P.Channel)

  // Scenario 2: Group Topic
  grpTopic := &Topic{
    name:      "grpSubTopic345",
    cat:       types.TopicCatGrp,
    xoriginal: "grpSubTopic345", // Should be used for Group
    lastID:    lastID,
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{}, // Needed for subsCount
  }
  receiptGrp := grpTopic.preparePushForSubReceipt(fromUid, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receiptGrp)
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receiptGrp.Payload.What)
  assert.False(t, receiptGrp.Payload.Silent)
  assert.Equal(t, grpTopic.xoriginal, receiptGrp.Payload.Topic) // Topic should be xoriginal for Group
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receiptGrp.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receiptGrp.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, lastID, receiptGrp.Payload.SeqId)
  assert.NotNil(t, receiptGrp.To)
  assert.Empty(t, receiptGrp.Channel)
}

func TestPushForP2PSub_Basic_678(t *testing.T) {
  fromUid := types.ParseUserId("usrP2PSender678")
  toUid := types.ParseUserId("usrP2PRcpt678")
  now := types.TimeNow()
  wantMode := types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite
  givenMode := types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite | types.ModePres

  p2pTopic := &Topic{
    cat:    types.TopicCatP2P,
    lastID: 7,
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{}, // Needed for subsCount
  }

  receipt := p2pTopic.pushForP2PSub(fromUid, toUid, wantMode, givenMode, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt)

  // Check recipient
  assert.Len(t, receipt.To, 1)
  _, ok := receipt.To[toUid]
  assert.True(t, ok)
  // Recipient struct is empty for subs
  assert.Equal(t, push.Recipient{}, receipt.To[toUid])

  // Check payload basics (inherited from preparePushForSubReceipt)
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receipt.Payload.What)
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receipt.Payload.Topic)
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receipt.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receipt.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, p2pTopic.lastID, receipt.Payload.SeqId)

  // Check specific P2P sub payload fields
  assert.Equal(t, wantMode, receipt.Payload.ModeWant)
  assert.Equal(t, givenMode, receipt.Payload.ModeGiven)
  assert.Empty(t, receipt.Channel)
}

import (

  "time"

  "github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"

  "github.com/tinode/chat/server/push"

Test Implementation:


func TestPushForGroupSub_Scenarios_901(t *testing.T) {
  topicName := "grpSubTopic901"
  fromUid := types.ParseUserId("usrGrpSender901")
  toUid1 := types.ParseUserId("usrGrpRcpt1") // Valid
  toUid2 := types.ParseUserId("usrGrpRcpt2") // Deleted
  toUid3 := types.ParseUserId("usrGrpRcpt3") // Invalid mode
  toUid4 := types.ParseUserId("usrGrpRcpt4") // Valid channel sub (should be excluded from To)

  now := types.TimeNow()
  wantMode := types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite
  givenMode := types.ModeRead | types.ModeWrite | types.ModePres

  tp := &Topic{
    name:      topicName,
    cat:       types.TopicCatGrp,
    xoriginal: topicName,
    lastID:    8,
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
      fromUid: { // Sender
        modeWant:  wantMode,
        modeGiven: givenMode,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid1: { // Valid recipient
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid2: { // Invalid: Deleted
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   true,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid3: { // Invalid: Not Reader/Presencer
        modeWant:  types.ModeWrite,
        modeGiven: types.ModeWrite,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
      },
      toUid4: { // Invalid: Is Channel sub
        modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    true,
      },
    },
  }

  // Scenario 1: Valid recipients exist
  receipt1 := tp.pushForGroupSub(fromUid, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt1)
  assert.Len(t, receipt1.To, 2) // fromUid, toUid1

  _, okSender := receipt1.To[fromUid]
  assert.True(t, okSender)
  _, ok1 := receipt1.To[toUid1]
  assert.True(t, ok1)
  _, ok2 := receipt1.To[toUid2]
  assert.False(t, ok2)
  _, ok3 := receipt1.To[toUid3]
  assert.False(t, ok3)
  _, ok4 := receipt1.To[toUid4]
  assert.False(t, ok4)

  // Check payload basics
  assert.Equal(t, push.ActSub, receipt1.Payload.What)
  assert.Equal(t, topicName, receipt1.Payload.Topic)
  assert.Equal(t, fromUid.UserId(), receipt1.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receipt1.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, tp.lastID, receipt1.Payload.SeqId)

  // Check specific Group sub payload fields (taken from sender's pud)
  assert.Equal(t, wantMode, receipt1.Payload.ModeWant)
  assert.Equal(t, givenMode, receipt1.Payload.ModeGiven)
  assert.Empty(t, receipt1.Channel)

  // Scenario 2: Sender not found in perUser (BUG case)
  tp.perUser = map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
    toUid1: tp.perUser[toUid1], // Keep one valid recipient
  }
  receipt2 := tp.pushForGroupSub(fromUid, now)
  assert.Nil(t, receipt2) // Should return nil if sender not found

  // Scenario 3: No valid recipients (excluding sender)
  tp.perUser = map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
     fromUid: { // Sender only
        modeWant:  wantMode,
        modeGiven: givenMode,
        online:    1,
        deleted:   false,
        isChan:    false,
     },
    toUid2: { // Deleted
      modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      online:    1, deleted: true, isChan: false,
    },
    toUid3: { // Invalid mode
      modeWant:  types.ModeWrite,
      modeGiven: types.ModeWrite,
      online:    1, deleted: false, isChan: false,
    },
     toUid4: { // Channel sub
      modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      online:    1, deleted: false, isChan: true,
    },
  }
  receipt3 := tp.pushForGroupSub(fromUid, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receipt3) // Should still return receipt if sender is present
  assert.Len(t, receipt3.To, 1) // Only sender
   _, okSender3 := receipt3.To[fromUid]
  assert.True(t, okSender3)


 // Scenario 4: No valid recipients at all (including sender)
  tp.perUser = map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
      fromUid: { // Sender with invalid mode
        modeWant:  types.ModeNone,
        modeGiven: types.ModeNone,
        online:    1, deleted: false, isChan: false,
     },
    toUid2: { // Deleted
      modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      online:    1, deleted: true, isChan: false,
    },
  }
   // Re-add sender with invalid mode before calling
   senderPud := tp.perUser[fromUid]
   receipt4 := tp.pushForGroupSub(fromUid, now)
   // Need to ensure the sender's pud exists *before* calling the function
   tp.perUser = map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{
      fromUid: { // Sender with invalid mode
        modeWant:  types.ModeNone,
        modeGiven: types.ModeNone,
        online:    1, deleted: false, isChan: false,
     },
    toUid2: { // Deleted
      modeWant:  types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      modeGiven: types.ModeRead | types.ModePres,
      online:    1, deleted: true, isChan: false,
    },
   }
   senderPud = tp.perUser[fromUid] // Ensure pud exists
   receipt4 = tp.pushForGroupSub(fromUid, now)
   require.NotNil(t, receipt4) // Prepare is called first
   assert.Empty(t, receipt4.To) // No valid recipients
   assert.Nil(t, receipt4) // Actually returns nil if To is empty and Channel is empty
}

func TestPushForReadRcpt_Scenarios_567(t *testing.T) {
  uid := types.ParseUserId("usrReader567")
  seq := 99
  now := types.TimeNow()

  // Scenario 1: P2P Topic
  p2pTopic := &Topic{
    cat:       types.TopicCatP2P,
    xoriginal: "usrOtherUser", // Should be ignored for P2P
     perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{}, // Needed for subsCount

  assert.Equal(t, push.ActRead, receiptP2P.Payload.What)

  assert.Equal(t, uid.UserId(), receiptP2P.Payload.From)

  assert.Empty(t, receiptP2P.Channel)

  // Scenario 2: Group Topic
  grpTopic := &Topic{
    name:      "grpReadTopic567",
    cat:       types.TopicCatGrp,
    xoriginal: "grpReadTopic567", // Should be used for Group
    perUser: map[types.Uid]*PerUserData{}, // Needed for subsCount
  }
  receiptGrp := grpTopic.pushForReadRcpt(uid, seq, now)
  require.NotNil(t, receiptGrp)

  assert.Len(t, receiptGrp.To, 1)
  _, okGrp := receiptGrp.To[uid]
  assert.True(t, okGrp)
  assert.Equal(t, push.Recipient{}, receiptGrp.To[uid]) // Empty recipient struct

  assert.Equal(t, push.ActRead, receiptGrp.Payload.What)
  assert.True(t, receiptGrp.Payload.Silent)
  assert.Equal(t, grpTopic.xoriginal, receiptGrp.Payload.Topic) // Topic is xoriginal for Group
  assert.Equal(t, uid.UserId(), receiptGrp.Payload.From)
  assert.Equal(t, now, receiptGrp.Payload.Timestamp)
  assert.Equal(t, seq, receiptGrp.Payload.SeqId)
  assert.Empty(t, receiptGrp.Channel)
}

func TestSendPush_NoCluster_112(t *testing.T) {
  setupGlobals(nil) // No cluster
  defer teardownGlobals()

  uid1 := types.ParseUserId("usrLocal112")
  channelName := "chnLocal112"

  // Scenario 1: Only 'To' recipients
  receipt1 := &push.Receipt{
    To: map[types.Uid]push.Recipient{
      uid1: {Delivered: 1},
    },
    Payload: push.Payload{What: "local_to"},
  }
  sendPush(receipt1)

  select {
  case req := <-globals.usersUpdate:
    require.NotNil(t, req.PushRcpt)
    assert.Equal(t, receipt1.Payload, req.PushRcpt.Payload)
    assert.Equal(t, receipt1.To, req.PushRcpt.To)
    assert.Empty(t, req.PushRcpt.Channel)
  case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
    t.Fatal("timed out waiting for usersUpdate channel")
  }

  // Scenario 2: Only 'Channel'
  receipt2 := &push.Receipt{
    Channel: channelName,
    Payload: push.Payload{What: "local_channel"},
  }
  sendPush(receipt2)

  select {
  case req := <-globals.usersUpdate:
    require.NotNil(t, req.PushRcpt)
    assert.Equal(t, receipt2.Payload, req.PushRcpt.Payload)
    assert.Equal(t, channelName, req.PushRcpt.Channel)
    // 'To' should be copied even if empty in original
    assert.NotNil(t, req.PushRcpt.To)
    assert.Empty(t, req.PushRcpt.To)
  case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
    t.Fatal("timed out waiting for usersUpdate channel")
  }

  // Scenario 3: Both 'To' and 'Channel'
  receipt3 := &push.Receipt{
     To: map[types.Uid]push.Recipient{
      uid1: {Delivered: 0},
    },
    Channel: channelName,
    Payload: push.Payload{What: "local_both"},
  }
  sendPush(receipt3)

   select {
  case req := <-globals.usersUpdate:
    require.NotNil(t, req.PushRcpt)
     assert.Equal(t, receipt3.Payload, req.PushRcpt.Payload)
     assert.Equal(t, receipt3.To, req.PushRcpt.To)
     assert.Equal(t, channelName, req.PushRcpt.Channel)
  case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
    t.Fatal("timed out waiting for usersUpdate channel")
  }


  // Scenario 4: Neither 'To' nor 'Channel' (should not send)
  receipt4 := &push.Receipt{
    Payload: push.Payload{What: "local_none"},
    To: map[types.Uid]push.Recipient{}, // Explicitly empty
  }
  sendPush(receipt4)

   select {
   case <-globals.usersUpdate:
       t.Fatal("usersUpdate channel should be empty for receipt with no To/Channel")
   case <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
       // OK - timed out as expected
   }
}

func TestSendPush_WithCluster_334(t *testing.T) {
  mockCluster := new(MockCluster)
  // Cluster setup needs globals.cluster to be assignable.
  // Assuming it's possible to assign a mock object satisfying the needed interface.
   setupGlobals(&Cluster{}) // Pass a placeholder non-nil cluster initially
   globals.cluster.node = mockCluster // Assign the mock part if Cluster embeds it, otherwise adjust
   // If globals.cluster is an interface:
   // setupGlobals(mockCluster)


  defer teardownGlobals()

  localUid := types.ParseUserId("usrLocal334")
  remoteUid1 := types.ParseUserId("usrRemote1_334")
  remoteUid2 := types.ParseUserId("usrRemote2_334")
  channelName := "chnCluster334"

  basePayload := push.Payload{What: "cluster_test"}

  // Mock cluster behavior
  mockCluster.On("isRemoteTopic", localUid.UserId()).Return(false)
  mockCluster.On("isRemoteTopic", remoteUid1.UserId()).Return(true)
  mockCluster.On("isRemoteTopic", remoteUid2.UserId()).Return(true)
  // Expect routeUserReq to be called with specific remote users/channel
  var capturedRemoteReq *UserCacheReq
  mockCluster.On("routeUserReq", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.UserCacheReq")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
      capturedRemoteReq = args.Get(0).(*UserCacheReq)
  }).Return()


  // Scenario 1: Mixed local/remote recipients, no channel
  receipt1 := &push.Receipt{
    To: map[types.Uid]push.Recipient{
      localUid:   {Delivered: 1},
      remoteUid1: {Delivered: 0},
      remoteUid2: {Delivered: 1},
    },
    Payload: basePayload,
  }
  sendPush(receipt1)

  // Check local dispatch
  select {
  case localReq := <-globals.usersUpdate:
    require.NotNil(t, localReq.PushRcpt)
    assert.Equal(t, basePayload, localReq.PushRcpt.Payload)
    assert.Len(t, localReq.PushRcpt.To, 1)
    _, okLocal := localReq.PushRcpt.To[localUid]
    assert.True(t, okLocal)
    assert.Empty(t, localReq.PushRcpt.Channel)
  case <-time.After(100 * time.Millisecond):
    t.Fatal("timed out waiting for local usersUpdate channel")
  }

  // Check remote dispatch
  mockCluster.AssertCalled(t, "routeUserReq", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.UserCacheReq"))
  require.NotNil(t, capturedRemoteReq)
  require.NotNil(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt)
  assert.Equal(t, basePayload, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.Payload)
  assert.Len(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.To, 2)
  _, okRemote1 := capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.To[remoteUid1]
  _, okRemote2 := capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.To[remoteUid2]
  assert.True(t, okRemote1)
  assert.True(t, okRemote2)
  assert.Empty(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.Channel)

  // Reset mock calls for next scenario
  mockCluster.Mock = mock.Mock{} // Reset calls
  mockCluster.On("isRemoteTopic", mock.Anything).Return(true) // Assume all remote for channel test
  capturedRemoteReq = nil // Reset capture
  mockCluster.On("routeUserReq", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.UserCacheReq")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
       capturedRemoteReq = args.Get(0).(*UserCacheReq)
  }).Return()


  // Scenario 2: Only Channel, no 'To' recipients
  receipt2 := &push.Receipt{
    Channel: channelName,
    Payload: basePayload,
     To: map[types.Uid]push.Recipient{}, // Ensure To is initialized but empty
  }
  sendPush(receipt2)

   // Check local dispatch (should NOT receive anything)
   select {
   case <-globals.usersUpdate:
       t.Fatal("usersUpdate channel should be empty when only channel is present")
   case <-time.After(50 * time.Millisecond):
       // OK
   }

  // Check remote dispatch (SHOULD be called for channel)
  mockCluster.AssertCalled(t, "routeUserReq", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.UserCacheReq"))
  require.NotNil(t, capturedRemoteReq)
  require.NotNil(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt)
  assert.Equal(t, basePayload, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.Payload)
  assert.Equal(t, channelName, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.Channel)
   assert.NotNil(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.To) // To should be initialized
  assert.Empty(t, capturedRemoteReq.PushRcpt.To)


  // Assert all mock expectations
  mockCluster.AssertExpectations(t)
}

// Mock for websocket.Conn
type mockWebsocketConn struct {
	mock.Mock
	// Store the pong handler to inspect/call it
	pongHandler func(string) error
	// Store read deadline
	readDeadline time.Time
	// Store write deadline
	writeDeadline time.Time
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) Close() error {
	args := m.Called()
	return args.Error(0)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) ReadMessage() (messageType int, p []byte, err error) {
	args := m.Called()
	// Handle nil return for p
	var pArg []byte
	if args.Get(1) != nil {
		pArg = args.Get(1).([]byte)
	}
	return args.Int(0), pArg, args.Error(2)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) WriteMessage(messageType int, data []byte) error {
	args := m.Called(messageType, data)
	return args.Error(0)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) SetReadLimit(limit int64) {
	m.Called(limit)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) SetReadDeadline(t time.Time) error {
	m.readDeadline = t // Store the deadline
	args := m.Called(mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")) // Use mock.AnythingOfType for time matching
	return args.Error(0)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) SetWriteDeadline(t time.Time) error {
	m.writeDeadline = t // Store the deadline
	args := m.Called(mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")) // Use mock.AnythingOfType for time matching
	return args.Error(0)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) SetPongHandler(h func(appData string) error) {
	m.pongHandler = h // Store the handler
	m.Called(mock.AnythingOfType("func(string) error")) // Use mock.AnythingOfType for function matching
}

// Helper methods to satisfy net.Conn interface embedded in websocket.Conn
func (m *mockWebsocketConn) Read(b []byte) (n int, err error) {
	args := m.Called(b)
	return args.Int(0), args.Error(1)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) Write(b []byte) (n int, err error) {
	args := m.Called(b)
	return args.Int(0), args.Error(1)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) LocalAddr() net.Addr {
	args := m.Called()
	return args.Get(0).(net.Addr)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) RemoteAddr() net.Addr {
	args := m.Called()
	// Handle potential nil return
	if args.Get(0) == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return args.Get(0).(net.Addr)
}

func (m *mockWebsocketConn) SetDeadline(t time.Time) error {
	args := m.Called(t)
	return args.Error(0)
}

	args := m.Called(conn, sid)
	// Allow returning nil session

	if args.Get(0) != nil {
		sess = args.Get(0).(*Session)
	}
	return sess, args.Int(1)
}

// Mock for websocket.Upgrader
type mockUpgrader struct {
	mock.Mock
}

func (m *mockUpgrader) Upgrade(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, responseHeader http.Header) (*websocket.Conn, error) {
	args := m.Called(w, r, responseHeader)
	// Allow returning nil connection
	var conn *websocket.Conn
	if args.Get(0) != nil {
		conn = args.Get(0).(*websocket.Conn)
	}
	return conn, args.Error(1)
}

// Mock for websocket.Dialer needed for some edge cases if client dialing is tested elsewhere
type mockDialer struct {
	mock.Mock
}

func (m *mockDialer) Dial(urlStr string, requestHeader http.Header) (*websocket.Conn, *http.Response, error) {
	args := m.Called(urlStr, requestHeader)
	// Allow returning nil connection/response
	var conn *websocket.Conn
	var resp *http.Response
	if args.Get(0) != nil {
		conn = args.Get(0).(*websocket.Conn)
	}
	if args.Get(1) != nil {
		resp = args.Get(1).(*http.Response)
	}
	return conn, resp, args.Error(2)
}

// --- Global Mocks & Helpers ---

var (
	// Mockable global functions/variables
	checkAPIKeyFunc  = checkAPIKey
	getAPIKeyFunc    = getAPIKey
	isRoutableIPFunc = isRoutableIP
	wsWriteFunc      = wsWrite // Use the original function by default

	// Stats mocking
	stats      = make(map[string]int64)
	statsMutex sync.Mutex
	statsInc   = func(name string, value int64) { // Assignable statsInc
		statsMutex.Lock()
		defer statsMutex.Unlock()
		stats[name] += value
	}
)

func resetStats() {
	statsMutex.Lock()
	defer statsMutex.Unlock()
	stats = make(map[string]int64)
}

func getStatsCount(name string) int64 {
	statsMutex.Lock()
	defer statsMutex.Unlock()
	return stats[name]
}

// Helper to setup global mocks for serveWebSocket tests
func setupServeWebSocketMocks(t *testing.T) (func(), *mockUpgrader, *mockSessionStore) {
	// Mock global functions
	originalCheckAPIKey := checkAPIKeyFunc
	originalGetAPIKey := getAPIKeyFunc
	originalIsRoutableIP := isRoutableIPFunc
	checkAPIKeyFunc = func(key string) (bool, time.Time) { return true, time.Time{} } // Default to valid
	getAPIKeyFunc = func(req *http.Request) string { return "validKey" }
	isRoutableIPFunc = func(ip string) bool { return true } // Default to routable

	// Mock upgrader
	originalUpgrader := upgrader
	mockUpgrader := new(mockUpgrader)
	upgrader = *mockUpgrader // Assign mock

	// Mock session store
	originalSessionStore := globals.sessionStore
	mockStore := new(mockSessionStore)
	globals.sessionStore = mockStore // Assign mock

	// Mock logger to avoid actual logging during tests
	originalLoggerInfo := logs.Info
	originalLoggerErr := logs.Err
	// logs.Init(io.Discard, "") // Use io.Discard if needed

	// Cleanup function
	cleanup := func() {
		checkAPIKeyFunc = originalCheckAPIKey
		getAPIKeyFunc = originalGetAPIKey
		isRoutableIPFunc = originalIsRoutableIP
		upgrader = originalUpgrader
		globals.sessionStore = originalSessionStore
		logs.Info = originalLoggerInfo
		logs.Err = originalLoggerErr
		wsWriteFunc = wsWrite // Restore original wsWrite
		// resetStats() // Optionally reset stats
	}

	return cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore
}

Import Statements:
import (
	"testing"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_SendMessage_QueueLimitExceeded_742(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	// Note: The logic in the original code checks `len(sess.send) > sendQueueLimit` *before*
	// attempting to send to the channel. This check seems incorrect as `len()` checks the number
	// of elements currently in the buffered channel, not its capacity or how many sends are pending.
	// The test simulates this potentially flawed check by pre-filling the buffer.
	// A channel with capacity sendQueueLimit will have len() == sendQueueLimit when full.
	// The check `len(sess.send) > sendQueueLimit` will thus likely never be true unless the
	// channel buffer size is misconfigured or the intent was different.
	// Assuming the intent was to limit concurrent pending sends rather than buffer size:
	// We can't directly test the `len(sess.send) > sendQueueLimit` condition easily without
	// complex concurrency simulation.
	// Let's test the scenario where the check *does* trigger, assuming it might in some context.
	sess := &Session{
		sid:  "testSid",
		// Create a channel *larger* than the limit to simulate the len() check potentially exceeding it.
        // This setup highlights the potential issue in the source code's check.
		send: make(chan interface{}, sendQueueLimit+5),
	}
	// Fill the channel so len() exceeds the limit
	for i := 0; i < sendQueueLimit+1; i++ {
		sess.send <- "dummy message"
	}
	require.Greater(t, len(sess.send), sendQueueLimit, "Channel length should exceed limit for this test scenario")

	// Act
	result := sess.sendMessage([]byte("test message"))

	// Assert
	assert.False(t, result, "sendMessage should return false when len(sess.send) > sendQueueLimit")
	// Ensure stats are not incremented when message is dropped due to the explicit queue length check
	assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal"), "Stats should not be incremented")
	// Note: Log check is omitted for brevity.
}

func TestSession_SendMessage_UnexpectedCloseError_974(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:  "testSid",
		ws:   mockWs,
		send: make(chan interface{}, 1),
	}
	msgBytes := []byte("test message")
	// Use an error code considered "unexpected" by IsUnexpectedCloseError
	expectedErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseInternalServerErr} // Example of unexpected

	// Mock wsWriteFunc
	originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
	wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
		return expectedErr // Simulate write failure with unexpected code
	}
	defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

	// Act
	result := sess.sendMessage(msgBytes)

	// Assert
	assert.False(t, result)
	assert.True(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(expectedErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure), "Error should be unexpected")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal")) // Stats incremented before write fails
	// Note: Log check for unexpected error could be added
}

import (
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
	"github.com/gorilla/websocket"

    "github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"
)

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_ReadLoop_SuccessAndPong_909(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)

	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1) // Wait for dispatchRaw to be called

	var dispatchedData []byte
	dispatchCalled := false
	cleanupCalled := false

	sess := &Session{
		sid: "testSid",
		ws:  mockWs,
		// Replace methods with test doubles
		dispatchRaw: func(rawData []byte) {
			dispatchedData = rawData
			dispatchCalled = true
			// Don't panic here, let the second ReadMessage call fail to exit loop naturally
			wg.Done() // Signal that dispatchRaw was called
		},
		cleanUp: func(evicted bool) {
			assert.False(t, evicted)
			cleanupCalled = true
		},
		// Mock closeWS to avoid nil pointer dereference if ws is nil after cleanup
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}

	msgBytes := []byte(`{"id":"123","hi":{"id":"hi123"}}`)
	readError := errors.New("read error to stop loop")

	// Configure mockWs expectations
	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Times(2) // Initial + after pong
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.AnythingOfType("func(string) error")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		// Simulate receiving a pong by calling the registered handler
		handler := args.Get(0).(func(string) error)
		err := handler("pong data")
		require.NoError(t, err, "Pong handler failed")
	}).Return().Once()
	// First call: return message
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(websocket.TextMessage, msgBytes, nil).Once()
	// Second call: return error to stop the loop
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, readError).Once()
	// Expect Close in the defer func
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	sess.readLoop() // Run the loop synchronously

	// Assert
	wg.Wait() // Ensure dispatchRaw was executed
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	assert.True(t, dispatchCalled, "dispatchRaw should have been called")
	assert.Equal(t, msgBytes, dispatchedData)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
	assert.True(t, cleanupCalled, "cleanUp should have been called")
	// Check if read deadline was updated after pong
	assert.NotZero(t, mockWs.readDeadline, "Read deadline should be set")
}

		ws:  mockWs,
		cleanUp: func(evicted bool) {
			assert.False(t, evicted, "Evicted should be false on read error")
			cleanupCalled = true
		},
         closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}

	// Use an error code considered "unexpected"
	closeErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseInternalServerErr, Text: "internal error"}

	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, closeErr).Once() // Return unexpected error
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Expected from defer

	// Act
	sess.readLoop()

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	assert.True(t, cleanupCalled, "cleanUp should be called")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal")) // No message processed
	// Implicitly testing logging by checking IsUnexpectedCloseError logic path
}

func TestSession_ReadLoop_NormalClose_378(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	cleanupCalled := false
	sess := &Session{
		sid: "testSid",
		ws:  mockWs,
		cleanUp: func(evicted bool) {
			assert.False(t, evicted)
			cleanupCalled = true
		},
         closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}

	// Use a normal close code (CloseNormalClosure or CloseGoingAway)
	closeErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseNormalClosure, Text: "client closed"}

	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Once()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Expected from defer

	// Act

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	assert.True(t, cleanupCalled)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
	// Implicitly testing no error logged by checking IsUnexpectedCloseError returns false
	assert.False(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(closeErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure))
}

import (

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendBatch_012(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 2), // Buffer for batch + stop
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour), // Non-firing timer
        serializeAndUpdateStats: func(msg *ServerComMessage) []byte { // Mock directly on struct
            if msg.Ctrl != nil {
                return []byte(msg.Ctrl.Id) // Simple serialization for test
            }
            return nil
        },
         // Mock closeWS to assert call
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
	defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	serializedMsg1 := []byte("msg1")
	serializedMsg2 := []byte("msg2")

    sendCount := 0
    // Mock wsWriteFunc for sendMessage verification
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg interface{}) bool {
        require.Equal(t, mockWs, ws)
        require.Equal(t, websocket.TextMessage, mt)
        sendCount++
        if sendCount == 1 {
            assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg1, msg.([]byte))
        } else {
            assert.Equal(t, serializedMsg2, msg.([]byte))
        }
        return true // Simulate successful send
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()


	// Replace sendMessage to use the mocked wsWriteFunc
    originalSendMessage := sess.sendMessage
    sess.sendMessage = func (msg any) bool {
        return wsWriteFunc(sess.ws, websocket.TextMessage, msg)
    }
    defer func() { sess.sendMessage = originalSendMessage }()


	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Expect close on exit

    // Act: Run writeLoop in a goroutine and send messages
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    // Send batch message
    msg1 := &ServerComMessage{Ctrl: &MsgServerCtrl{Id: "msg1"}}
    msg2 := &ServerComMessage{Ctrl: &MsgServerCtrl{Id: "msg2"}}
    sess.send <- []*ServerComMessage{msg1, msg2}

    // Stop the loop
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Give time for send to be processed
    sess.stop <- nil
    wg.Wait() // Wait for writeLoop to exit

	// Assert
	assert.Equal(t, 2, sendCount, "Both messages should be sent")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendMessageFail_011(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	serializedMsg := []byte("serialized data")
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1), // Only need space for one message
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),      // Stop channel might not be needed if loop exits on fail
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	sendAttempted := false
    // Mock wsWriteFunc to simulate failure
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg interface{}) bool {
         sendAttempted = true
         return false // Simulate send failure
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

    // Replace sendMessage
    originalSendMessage := sess.sendMessage
    sess.sendMessage = func (msg any) bool {
         return wsWriteFunc(sess.ws, websocket.TextMessage, msg)
    }
    defer func() { sess.sendMessage = originalSendMessage }()


	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Should close on exit

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    sess.send <- serializedMsg
    // Loop should exit on send failure, wait for it
    wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, sendAttempted, "Should attempt to send")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t) // Verifies Close was called
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_BackgroundTimer_372(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockBkgTimerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	onBackgroundTimerCalled := false

	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  &time.Timer{C: mockBkgTimerChan}, // Use controlled channel
		background: true,                             // Start in background
        onBackgroundTimer: func() {                   // Mock directly
            onBackgroundTimerCalled = true
        },
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
	// Do not stop this timer as we control the channel directly

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    mockBkgTimerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger background timer
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow time for processing
    sess.stop <- nil                 // Stop the loop
    wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, onBackgroundTimerCalled, "onBackgroundTimer should have been called")
	assert.False(t, sess.background, "background flag should be false after timer")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}
