func TestSession_WriteLoop_StopWithMessage_991(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	stopMsg := []byte("stopping")
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Use []byte type as in source
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg := false
    // Mock wsWriteFunc for the stop message and pings
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
        if mt == websocket.TextMessage {
            if bytes, ok := msg.([]byte); ok && string(bytes) == string(stopMsg) {
                wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg = true
            }
        } else if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
            // Allow pings to succeed silently in this test
            return nil
        }
        return nil // Assume other writes succeed
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    sess.stop <- stopMsg // Send stop signal with message
    wg.Wait()           // Wait for loop to exit

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, wsWriteCalledWithStopMsg, "wsWriteFunc should be called with the stop message")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}

import (
    "errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
	"github.com/gorilla/websocket"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
     "github.com/tinode/chat/server/store/types"

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingSuccess_681(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1) // Controlled ticker channel

	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Use []byte type
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		// bkgTimer:  Must be initialized, but we won't trigger it. Use a real one.
        bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour), // Real timer, won't fire
         closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop() // Ensure timer is stopped


	pingWriteCalled := false
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
        if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
            pingWriteCalled = true
            assert.Nil(t, msg)
            return nil // Simulate successful ping write
        }
        // Ignore TextMessage writes for simplicity
        if mt == websocket.TextMessage {
            return nil
        }
        return errors.New("unexpected write") // Fail on other unexpected writes
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

    // Mock time.NewTicker to return our controlled channel
    originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
    time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
        assert.Equal(t, pingPeriod, d) // Verify correct period is used
        return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
    }
    defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    mockTickerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger ticker
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow time for ping processing
    sess.stop <- nil               // Stop the loop
    wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, pingWriteCalled, "Ping write should have been called")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingFailNormal_883(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	expectedErr := errors.New("ping write failed - normal error")
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Use []byte type
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
         closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	pingWriteAttempted := false
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
        if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
            pingWriteAttempted = true
            return expectedErr // Simulate ping write failure (normal error)
        }
        if mt == websocket.TextMessage { return nil } // Ignore text messages
        return nil
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

    originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
    time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
        return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
    }
    defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Should close on exit after error

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    mockTickerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger ticker
    // Loop should exit on ping error, wait for it
    wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, pingWriteAttempted)
	assert.False(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(expectedErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure), "Error should not be unexpected")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t) // Verifies Close was called
	// Log check for non-unexpected error omitted
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingFailUnexpected_463(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	// Use an unexpected error code
	expectedErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.ClosePolicyViolation}
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1), // Use []byte type
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
        closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	pingWriteAttempted := false
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
        if mt == websocket.PingMessage {
            pingWriteAttempted = true
            return expectedErr // Simulate unexpected close error on ping
        }
        if mt == websocket.TextMessage { return nil }
        return nil
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()

    originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
    time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
        return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
    }
    defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Should close on exit after error

    // Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        defer wg.Done()
        sess.writeLoop()
    }()

    mockTickerChan <- time.Now() // Trigger ticker
    wg.Wait()                   // Wait for loop to exit on error

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, pingWriteAttempted)
	assert.True(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(expectedErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure), "Error should be unexpected")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	// Log check for unexpected error could be added
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendChannelClosed_918(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1), // Channel to be closed
		stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),      // Use []byte type
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
         closeWS: func() {
            mockWs.Close()
        },
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()


    // Mock time.NewTicker to avoid interference
    originalNewTicker := time.NewTicker
    mockTickerChan := make(chan time.Time) // Block ticker
    time.NewTicker = func(d time.Duration) *time.Ticker {
        return &time.Ticker{C: mockTickerChan}
    }
    defer func() { time.NewTicker = originalNewTicker }()


	// Mock wsWriteFunc to ensure no messages are attempted
    originalWsWrite := wsWriteFunc
    writeAttempted := false
    wsWriteFunc = func(ws *websocket.Conn, mt int, msg any) error {
        writeAttempted = true
        return nil
    }
    defer func() { wsWriteFunc = originalWsWrite }()


	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once() // Expect close on exit

    // Close the send channel before starting the loop
    close(sess.send)

    // Act
    // Run writeLoop synchronously because it should exit immediately
    sess.writeLoop()

	// Assert
	assert.False(t, writeAttempted, "No write should be attempted if send channel is closed")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t) // Verifies Close was called
}

	defer cleanup()

	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	// Override default mock behavior for this test

	assert.Contains(t, rr.Body.String(), `"code":`+assert.Sprintf("%d", http.StatusForbidden))

}

func TestServeWebSocket_UpgradeHandshakeError_663(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, _ := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
	// Use a ResponseRecorder that captures the Hijacked connection if needed,
	// though for handshake errors, Upgrade usually writes the error itself.
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	// API key is valid (default mock behavior)

	// Simulate handshake error
	// Note: The HandshakeError struct has unexported fields, making it hard to create directly.
	// We'll use a generic error and check if the code correctly identifies it as HandshakeError type.
	// In a real scenario, gorilla/websocket library creates this error internally.
	// We simulate the *behavior* of Upgrade returning this *type* of error.
	handshakeErr := websocket.HandshakeError{Description: "test handshake error"} // Description is exported

	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(nil, handshakeErr).Once()

	// Act
	serveWebSocket(rr, req)

	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	// In case of handshake error, the Upgrade function itself usually writes the
	// HTTP error response (e.g., 400 Bad Request). Our handler should just log and return.
	// We don't assert rr.Code as it's handled by the mockUpgrader's simulated behavior.
	// We primarily verify that the function exited after the failed upgrade attempt.
	// Log check omitted.
}

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_NoXFF_451(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
	req.RemoteAddr = "192.0.2.1:12345" // Set remote addr
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder() // Use ResponseRecorder for Upgrade

	globals.useXForwardedFor = false // Ensure XFF is disabled

	// Mock websocket connection
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
    // Upgrade needs a non-nil *websocket.Conn
    // We pass our mock, but cast it appropriately if needed by the mock setup.
    // For testify mocks, passing the interface type usually works.
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return((*websocket.Conn)(mockWs), nil).Once()

	// Mock session creation
	sess := &Session{
        sid:       "newSessionID",
        ws:        mockWs, // Assign the mock connection to the session
        // Initialize channels and timers to avoid nil panics if loops were real
        send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        stop:      make(chan []byte, 1),
        detach:    make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour), // Use a real timer
        // Setup mocks for methods called by the loops/cleanup
        closeWS: func() { mockWs.Close() }, // Ensure mock Close is called
        cleanUp: func(evicted bool){},      // Dummy cleanup
        dispatchRaw: func(rawData []byte){}, // Dummy dispatch
    }
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop() // Ensure timer cleanup

	expectedCount := 5
	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.Anything, "").Return(sess, expectedCount) // Use mock.Anything for conn

    // Mock the loops to prevent them running infinitely. We just check they are called.
    // This requires making writeLoop and readLoop fields/variables or using DI.
    // Simpler approach for now: Don't run the loops, just verify session setup.
    // Let the goroutines start, but they won't do much with a mock connection.

    mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil) // Expect Close from loop deferrals if they were real

	// Act
    // Run serveWebSocket and wait briefly for goroutines to potentially start
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1) // Add waitgroup counter
    go func() {
         serveWebSocket(rr, req)
         wg.Done() // Signal completion of serveWebSocket execution
    }()
    // Give goroutines *some* chance to start, though verifying their execution
    // without complex mocking or actual server setup is hard.
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)

	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)

	// Verify session properties
	require.NotNil(t, sess)
	assert.Equal(t, req.RemoteAddr, sess.remoteAddr)
    assert.Equal(t, "newSessionID", sess.sid)

    // Since we can't easily assert the loops ran without more complex mocking/DI,
    // we focus on the setup part of serveWebSocket.
    // To truly test loops, they'd need separate tests controlling their inputs/mocks.

    // Stop the main goroutine (which started the loops in the real code)
    // In this test setup, the loops aren't actually running meaningfully
    // Send a stop signal to potentially terminate the writeLoop goroutine if it started.
     // (This assumes the loops were actually started and are waiting)
    // select {
    // case sess.stop <- nil:
    // default: // Avoid blocking if loop already exited or never started properly
    // }

    wg.Wait() // Wait for the main serveWebSocket goroutine to finish its setup

}

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_XFF_Routable_902(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	xffAddr := "203.0.113.5" // Routable example
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", xffAddr)
	req.RemoteAddr = "192.0.2.1:12345"
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	globals.useXForwardedFor = true // Enable XFF
	// isRoutableIPFunc remains default true

	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return((*websocket.Conn)(mockWs), nil).Once()

	sess := &Session{ sid: "newSessionID", ws: mockWs,
        // Dummy channels/timers/methods
        send: make(chan interface{}, 1), stop: make(chan []byte, 1), detach: make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer: time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour),
        closeWS: func() { mockWs.Close() }, cleanUp: func(evicted bool){}, dispatchRaw: func(rawData []byte){},
    }
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.Anything, "").Return(sess, 1)
    mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil)

	// Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        serveWebSocket(rr, req)
        wg.Done()
    }()
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow goroutine setup


	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)
	require.NotNil(t, sess)
	assert.Equal(t, xffAddr, sess.remoteAddr) // Should use XFF header

    wg.Wait() // Wait for serveWebSocket goroutine to finish
}

import (

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_XFF_NonRoutable_538(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	xffAddr := "192.168.1.100" // Non-routable example
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", xffAddr)
	req.RemoteAddr = "192.0.2.1:12345"
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	globals.useXForwardedFor = true // Enable XFF
	isRoutableIPFunc = func(ip string) bool { return false } // Simulate non-routable IP

	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return((*websocket.Conn)(mockWs), nil).Once()

	sess := &Session{ sid: "newSessionID", ws: mockWs,
         // Dummy channels/timers/methods
        send: make(chan interface{}, 1), stop: make(chan []byte, 1), detach: make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer: time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour),
        closeWS: func() { mockWs.Close() }, cleanUp: func(evicted bool){}, dispatchRaw: func(rawData []byte){},
    }
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.Anything, "").Return(sess, 1)
    mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil)


	// Act
    var wg sync.WaitGroup
    wg.Add(1)
    go func() {
        serveWebSocket(rr, req)
        wg.Done()
    }()
    time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow goroutine setup

	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)
	require.NotNil(t, sess)
	assert.Equal(t, req.RemoteAddr, sess.remoteAddr) // Should fall back to RemoteAddr

    wg.Wait() // Wait for serveWebSocket goroutine to finish
}

import (

Required Library Installation
# No external libraries needed beyond testify and gorilla/websocket

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_SendMessage_QueueLimitExceeded_505(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	sess := &Session{
		sid: "testSid",
		// Create a channel with capacity > sendQueueLimit to make the len check possible
		send: make(chan interface{}, sendQueueLimit+5),
		ws:   new(mockWebsocketConn), // Assign a mock WS even if not used directly
	}
	// Fill the channel so len() > sendQueueLimit
	for i := 0; i < sendQueueLimit+1; i++ {
		// Use non-blocking send for setup
		select {
		case sess.send <- "dummy":
		default:
			t.Fatal("Failed to fill channel for test setup")
		}
	}
	require.Greater(t, len(sess.send), sendQueueLimit, "Channel length must exceed limit for this test")

	// Act
	result := sess.sendMessage([]byte("message that should be dropped"))

	// Assert
	assert.False(t, result, "sendMessage should return false when len(sess.send) > sendQueueLimit")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal"), "Stats should not be incremented")
	// Ensure wsWrite was not called
	sess.ws.(*mockWebsocketConn).AssertNotCalled(t, "WriteMessage", mock.Anything, mock.Anything)
}

func TestSession_SendMessage_WriteUnexpectedCloseError_707(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:  "testSid",
		ws:   mockWs,
		send: make(chan interface{}, 1),
	}
	msgBytes := []byte("test message")
	// Use an error code considered "unexpected" by IsUnexpectedCloseError
	expectedErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseInternalServerErr} // Example of unexpected

	// Mock wsWrite to fail with the unexpected error
	mockWs.On("SetWriteDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("WriteMessage", websocket.TextMessage, msgBytes).Return(expectedErr).Once()

	// Act
	result := sess.sendMessage(msgBytes)

	// Assert
	assert.False(t, result)
	assert.True(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(expectedErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure), "Error should be unexpected")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal")) // Stats incremented before write fails
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	// Note: Log check for unexpected error could be added
}

import (

Required Library Installation
# No external libraries needed beyond testify and gorilla/websocket

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_ReadLoop_SuccessAndPong_808(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)

	dispatchCalled := false
	var dispatchedData []byte
	cleanupCalled := false

	// Mock session methods indirectly or check side effects
	originalStatsInc := statsInc
	statsInc = func(name string, value int64) {
		if name == "IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal" {
			dispatchCalled = true // Assume dispatch happens if stats are incremented
		}
		originalStatsInc(name, value)
	}
	defer func() { statsInc = originalStatsInc }()


	// Mock hub.unregister as a proxy for sess.cleanUp being called
    originalHubUnregister := hub.unregister
    hub.unregister = func(s *Session, evicted bool) {
         assert.False(t, evicted, "Evicted should be false")
         cleanupCalled = true
    }
    defer func() { hub.unregister = originalHubUnregister }()


	sess := &Session{
		sid:  "testSid",
		ws:   mockWs,
		// other fields like send, stop, detach, bkgTimer, topicSub, userInfo etc. might need initialization
        // depending on what cleanUp and dispatchRaw actually do. Assume they are safe with nil/zero values for now.
        send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1), // Use interface{} for stop channel based on writeLoop usage
        detach:    make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour), // Non-firing timer
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()


	msgBytes := []byte(`{"id":"123","hi":{"id":"hi123"}}`) // Sample message
	readError := errors.New("read error to stop loop")

	// Configure mockWs expectations
	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	// Expect two deadline sets: initial and after pong
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Times(2)
	// Capture and call the pong handler
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.AnythingOfType("func(string) error")).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		handler := args.Get(0).(func(string) error)
		err := handler("pong data") // Simulate receiving a pong
		require.NoError(t, err, "Pong handler failed")
	}).Return().Once()

	// First ReadMessage returns data
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(websocket.TextMessage, msgBytes, nil).Once().Run(func(args mock.Arguments){
        // Copy the dispatched data when ReadMessage succeeds
         dispatchedData = msgBytes
    })
	// Second ReadMessage returns error to exit the loop
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, readError).Once()
	// Expect Close() in the defer func() within readLoop
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	sess.readLoop() // Run the loop synchronously

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	assert.True(t, dispatchCalled, "dispatchRaw side effect (stats inc) should have happened")
	assert.Equal(t, msgBytes, dispatchedData, "Dispatched data should match read data")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal"))
	assert.True(t, cleanupCalled, "cleanUp side effect (hub.unregister) should have happened")
	// Check if read deadline was updated after pong (implicitly checked by Times(2) on SetReadDeadline)
}

func TestSession_ReadLoop_UnexpectedClose_909(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	cleanupCalled := false

    // Mock hub.unregister as a proxy for sess.cleanUp being called
    originalHubUnregister := hub.unregister
    hub.unregister = func(s *Session, evicted bool) {
         assert.False(t, evicted, "Evicted should be false on read error")
         cleanupCalled = true
    }
    defer func() { hub.unregister = originalHubUnregister }()


	sess := &Session{
		sid: "testSid",
		ws:  mockWs,
        // Initialize channels etc.
        send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        detach:    make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	// Use an error code considered "unexpected"
	closeErr := &websocket.CloseError{Code: websocket.CloseInternalServerErr, Text: "internal error"}

	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, closeErr).Once() // Return unexpected error
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()                   // Expected from defer

	// Act
	sess.readLoop()

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	assert.True(t, cleanupCalled, "cleanUp side effect (hub.unregister) should be called")
	assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("IncomingMessagesWebsockTotal")) // No message processed
	// Implicitly testing logging by checking IsUnexpectedCloseError logic path
	assert.True(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(closeErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure))
}

func TestSession_ReadLoop_NormalClose_010(t *testing.T) {

	resetStats()

	cleanupCalled := false

    // Mock hub.unregister as a proxy for sess.cleanUp being called
    originalHubUnregister := hub.unregister
    hub.unregister = func(s *Session, evicted bool) {
         assert.False(t, evicted)
         cleanupCalled = true
    }

	sess := &Session{
		sid: "testSid",

         // Initialize channels etc.
        send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
        detach:    make(chan string, 1),
        bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockWs.On("SetReadLimit", globals.maxMessageSize).Return().Once()
	mockWs.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Once()

		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1), // Stop channel (use interface{} type based on select case)
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour), // Non-firing timer
	}
	defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	// Simple serialization for testing
	msg1 := &ServerComMessage{Ctrl: &MsgServerCtrl{Id: "ctrl1", Code: 200, Text: "OK1"}}
	msg2 := &ServerComMessage{Data: &MsgServerData{Topic: "test", SeqId: 1}}
	serializedMsg1, _ := json.Marshal(msg1)
	serializedMsg2, _ := json.Marshal(msg2)

	// Mock successful writes for both messages
	mockWs.On("SetWriteDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Times(2)
	mockWs.On("WriteMessage", websocket.TextMessage, serializedMsg1).Return(nil).Once()
	mockWs.On("WriteMessage", websocket.TextMessage, serializedMsg2).Return(nil).Once()
	// Expect close on exit
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act: Run writeLoop in a goroutine and send messages
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop()
	}()

	// Send batch message
	sess.send <- []*ServerComMessage{msg1, msg2}

	// Stop the loop
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Give time for send to be processed
	sess.stop <- nil
	wg.Wait() // Wait for writeLoop to exit

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
    // Check stats incremented twice (once per message in batch via serializeAndUpdateStats)
    assert.Equal(t, int64(2), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesTotal"), "Stats OutgoingMessagesTotal mismatch")
    assert.Equal(t, int64(2), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal"), "Stats OutgoingMessagesWebsockTotal mismatch")
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendSerialized_333(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	resetStats()
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	serializedMsg := []byte(`{"data":{"topic":"test"}}`)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 2),
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	// Mock successful write
	mockWs.On("SetWriteDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once()

	// Act

	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop()
	}()

	sess.send <- serializedMsg
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
	sess.stop <- nil
	wg.Wait()

    assert.Equal(t, int64(0), getStatsCount("OutgoingMessagesTotal"), "Stats OutgoingMessagesTotal should not be incremented for serialized")

func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendMessageFail_444(t *testing.T) {

	wg.Wait()

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_BackgroundTimer_555(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockBkgTimerChan := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	onBackgroundTimerCalled := false

    // Mock hub.markSessionBackground as a proxy for onBackgroundTimer
    originalHubMarkBg := hub.markSessionBackground
    hub.markSessionBackground = func(s *Session, background bool) {
        if !background { // onBackgroundTimer sets it to false
             onBackgroundTimerCalled = true
        }
    }
    defer func(){ hub.markSessionBackground = originalHubMarkBg }()


	sess := &Session{
		sid:        "testSid",
		ws:         mockWs,
		send:       make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:       make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:     make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:   &time.Timer{C: mockBkgTimerChan}, // Use controlled channel
		background: true,                             // Start in background
	}
	// Do not stop this timer as we control the channel directly

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop()
	}()

	mockBkgTimerChan <- time.Now()    // Trigger background timer
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow time for processing
	sess.stop <- nil                  // Stop the loop
	wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	assert.True(t, onBackgroundTimerCalled, "onBackgroundTimer side effect (hub.markSessionBackground) should have been called")
	assert.False(t, sess.background, "background flag should be false after timer")
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_Detach_888(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	topicToDetach := "grpTopic123"
    delSubCalled := false


    // Mock hub.delSubscription as proxy for sess.delSub
    originalHubDelSub := hub.delSubscription
    hub.delSubscription = func (s *Session, topic string, unsub bool) {
         if topic == topicToDetach {
              delSubCalled = true
         }
    }
    defer func() { hub.delSubscription = originalHubDelSub }()

	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
		// Initialize subs map if delSub accesses it
		subs:      make(map[string]*Subscription),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop()
	}()

	sess.detach <- topicToDetach       // Send detach signal
	time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond) // Allow time for processing
	sess.stop <- nil                  // Stop the loop
	wg.Wait()

	// Assert

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingSuccess_999(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	// Use a very short ping period for testing
	originalPingPeriod := pingPeriod
	pingPeriod = 10 * time.Millisecond
	defer func() { pingPeriod = originalPingPeriod }()

	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	pingSent := make(chan bool, 1)

	// Mock successful ping write
	mockWs.On("SetWriteDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Maybe() // May be called for messages too
	mockWs.On("WriteMessage", websocket.PingMessage, mock.Anything).Return(nil).Run(func(args mock.Arguments) {
		// Signal that ping was sent. Use non-blocking send.
		select {
		case pingSent <- true:
		default:
		}
	}).Once() // Expect at least one ping
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop()
	}()

	// Wait for ping or timeout
	select {
	case <-pingSent:
		// Ping received, proceed to stop
	case <-time.After(200 * time.Millisecond): // Timeout increased
		t.Fatal("Timed out waiting for ping message")
	}

	sess.stop <- nil // Stop the loop
	wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
}

func TestSession_WriteLoop_PingFailNormal_001(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	originalPingPeriod := pingPeriod
	pingPeriod = 10 * time.Millisecond
	defer func() { pingPeriod = originalPingPeriod }()

	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	expectedErr := errors.New("ping write failed - normal error")
	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	// Mock ping write failure
	mockWs.On("SetWriteDeadline", mock.AnythingOfType("time.Time")).Return(nil).Once() // Should be called before WriteMessage
	mockWs.On("WriteMessage", websocket.PingMessage, mock.Anything).Return(expectedErr).Once()
	// Expect Close because loop should exit on ping failure
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		sess.writeLoop() // Should exit due to ping failure
	}()

	// Loop should exit on ping error, wait for it
	wg.Wait()

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t) // Verifies WriteMessage and Close were called
	assert.False(t, websocket.IsUnexpectedCloseError(expectedErr, websocket.CloseGoingAway, websocket.CloseAbnormalClosure, websocket.CloseNormalClosure), "Error should not be unexpected")
	// Log check for non-unexpected error omitted
}

Test Implementation:
func TestSession_WriteLoop_SendChannelClosed_003(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	mockWs := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	sendChan := make(chan interface{}, 1)
	close(sendChan) // Close the channel before starting

	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "testSid",
		ws:        mockWs,
		send:      sendChan, // Use the closed channel
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	// Expect Close because loop exits
	mockWs.On("Close").Return(nil).Once()

	// Act
	// Run writeLoop synchronously because it should exit immediately
	sess.writeLoop()

	// Assert
	mockWs.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockWs.AssertNotCalled(t, "WriteMessage", mock.Anything, mock.Anything)
}

func TestServeWebSocket_UpgradeHandshakeError_006(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, _ := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws?apikey=validKey", nil)
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	// Use default valid API key mock

	// Simulate handshake error
	// The actual HandshakeError struct is complex; we simulate the type check.
	handshakeErr := websocket.HandshakeError{Description: "test handshake error"} // Use exported field

	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(nil, handshakeErr).Once()

	// Act
	serveWebSocket(rr, req)

	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	// In case of handshake error, the Upgrade function itself writes the response.
	// Our handler should just log and return. We check the log implicitly.
	// Asserting rr.Code might be unreliable as Upgrade handles it.
	// If logs were captured, we'd check for "ws: Not a websocket handshake".
}

--------------------Test Case--------------------
Import Statements:
import (
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"
    "errors"
    "sync"


	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/mock"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
    "github.com/gorilla/websocket" // Needed for mocks
)

Required Library Installation
# No external libraries needed beyond testify and gorilla/websocket

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_NoXFF_008(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	remoteAddr := "192.0.2.1:12345"
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws?apikey=validKey", nil)
	req.RemoteAddr = remoteAddr // Set remote addr
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	globals.useXForwardedFor = false // Ensure XFF is disabled

	mockWsConn := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(mockWsConn, nil).Once()

	sess := &Session{
		sid:       "newSessionID",
		ws:        mockWsConn,
		// Initialize channels and timers to prevent panics in goroutines
		send:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		stop:      make(chan interface{}, 1),
		detach:    make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer:  time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour), // Use a real timer
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop() // Ensure timer cleanup

	expectedCount := 5
    // Use mock.AnythingOfType because the type of the connection passed might be *mockWebsocketConn
	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.mockWebsocketConn"), "").Return(sess, expectedCount).Once()

    // Mock methods called by loops to prevent actual execution/panics
    // We assume loops start but don't test their full logic here.
    mockWsConn.On("Close").Return(nil).Maybe() // Loops might call Close on exit
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadLimit", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.Anything).Return(nil).Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, errors.New("stop loop")).Maybe() // Prevent infinite loop


	// Act
	serveWebSocket(rr, req)
    // Allow tiny delay for goroutines to potentially start
    time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)


	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)

	// Verify session properties set by serveWebSocket
	require.NotNil(t, sess) // sess must be populated by the mockStore return
	assert.Equal(t, remoteAddr, sess.remoteAddr)
	assert.Equal(t, "newSessionID", sess.sid)

    // Signal stop to potentially terminate writeLoop if it started
    select {
    case sess.stop <- nil:
    default:
    }
}

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_XFF_Routable_009(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	xffAddr := "203.0.113.5" // Routable example
	remoteAddr := "192.0.2.1:12345"
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws?apikey=validKey", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", xffAddr)
	req.RemoteAddr = remoteAddr
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	globals.useXForwardedFor = true // Enable XFF
	// isRoutableIP remains default true from setup

	mockWsConn := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(mockWsConn, nil).Once()

	sess := &Session{
		sid: "newSessionID", ws: mockWsConn,
		send: make(chan interface{}, 1), stop: make(chan interface{}, 1), detach: make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer: time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour),
	}
    defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.mockWebsocketConn"), "").Return(sess, 1).Once()
    mockWsConn.On("Close").Return(nil).Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadLimit", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.Anything).Return(nil).Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, errors.New("stop loop")).Maybe()


	// Act
	serveWebSocket(rr, req)
    time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)

	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)
	require.NotNil(t, sess)
	assert.Equal(t, xffAddr, sess.remoteAddr) // Should use XFF header

    select { case sess.stop <- nil: default: }
}

Test Implementation:
func TestServeWebSocket_Success_XFF_NonRoutable_011(t *testing.T) {
	// Arrange
	cleanup, mockUpgrader, mockStore := setupServeWebSocketMocks(t)
	defer cleanup()

	xffAddr := "192.168.1.100" // Non-routable example
	remoteAddr := "192.0.2.1:12345"
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/ws?apikey=validKey", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", xffAddr)
	req.RemoteAddr = remoteAddr
	rr := httptest.NewRecorder()

	globals.useXForwardedFor = true // Enable XFF
	isRoutableIP = func(ip string) bool { // Simulate non-routable IP
		assert.Equal(t, xffAddr, ip)
		return false
	}

	mockWsConn := new(mockWebsocketConn)
	mockUpgrader.On("Upgrade", rr, req, http.Header(nil)).Return(mockWsConn, nil).Once()

	sess := &Session{
		sid: "newSessionID", ws: mockWsConn,
		send: make(chan interface{}, 1), stop: make(chan interface{}, 1), detach: make(chan string, 1),
		bkgTimer: time.NewTimer(1 * time.Hour),
	}
     defer sess.bkgTimer.Stop()

	mockStore.On("NewSession", mock.AnythingOfType("*main.mockWebsocketConn"), "").Return(sess, 1).Once()
    mockWsConn.On("Close").Return(nil).Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadLimit", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetReadDeadline", mock.Anything).Return(nil).Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("SetPongHandler", mock.Anything).Return().Maybe()
    mockWsConn.On("ReadMessage").Return(0, nil, errors.New("stop loop")).Maybe()


	// Act
	serveWebSocket(rr, req)
    time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)


	// Assert
	mockUpgrader.AssertExpectations(t)
	mockStore.AssertExpectations(t)
	require.NotNil(t, sess)
	assert.Equal(t, remoteAddr, sess.remoteAddr) // Should fall back to RemoteAddr

    select { case sess.stop <- nil: default: }
}
